​臺灣勞工健康檢查交換實作指引 (Taiwan Labor Health Examination Exchange FHIR IG, TWHA IG), published by 衛生福利部次世代數位醫療平臺專案辦公室 & 長庚醫療財團法人長庚紀念醫院. This guide is not an authorized publication; it is the continuous build for version 0.6.1 built by the FHIR (HL7® FHIR® Standard) CI Build. This version is based on the current content of https://github.com/kunjulin/occupationIG and changes regularly. See the Directory of published versions
